## 8o. Shopify shop (26418699, Winstar4U) #1,261-1,360 — tenth full-visual-inspection batch: 100/100
## visually inspected, 21/100 confirmed defects, 0 confirmed duplicate pairs, 3 brand-safety flags
## (recurrence of the §8n alcohol-on-kids-blueprint pattern), NEW systemic finding: Wine Tumbler
## engraving legibility defect class (2026-11-18, this session)

**Scope:** products ranked #1,261-1,360 by Shopify catalog list order in shop 26418699, enumerated via
`printify-get-products-9eb` list pages (positions 1,251-1,400) and sliced to the exact 100-product
range. Blueprint mix: Youth Hoodie (20), Winter Scarf (21), Wine Tumbler (21), Duvet Cover (14), Cutting
Board (14), Shot Glass (7), Shower Curtain (3). Bridge task T-6I505W304E.

### CONFIRMED DEFECTS: 21/100

**A) Youth Hoodie — 8/20 defective, split across two sub-patterns:**

1. **Brand-safety / content-mismatch (3 products)** — alcohol/cocktail-themed artwork originally
   authored for adult drinkware (Wine Tumbler, Shot Glass) was ALSO mapped onto the Youth Hoodie
   blueprint, which Printify markets as a children's/kids garment ("Cozy Kids Holiday Hoodie" in every
   title in this family):
   - `6ac711cec077332834034d2e` "Christmas Open Bar" Youth Hoodie — text "I'M JUST FOR THE OPEN BAR"
     with a cocktail-glass graphic, identical source file (`openbar_fixed.png`) also used on the Wine
     Tumbler version of this design.
   - `6ac711ab336f8358660114d1` "Chic Happens Cocktail" Youth Hoodie — "CHIC HAPPENS — HOLIDAY COCKTAIL
     HOUR" with a martini-glass graphic.
   - `6ac7118fc077332834034cfd` "Naughty List Skeleton V2" Youth Hoodie — a skeleton holding a cocktail
     glass with the text "Maybe I liked being on the naughty list."
   This is a DIRECT RECURRENCE of the pattern first found in the prior batch (§8n), which flagged 3
   alcohol-themed Youth Hoodie designs there too — the running total is now 6 confirmed instances across
   two consecutive batches, indicating this is systemic (the same design catalog appears to be applied
   to every blueprint, including Youth Hoodie, without a content-appropriateness check). **Flagged for
   owner review — not resolved by this inspection.** Several other Youth Hoodie designs with Santa/skeleton
   imagery or workplace-snark humor (e.g. "Naughty List Resume," "Bah Humbug Wine") were checked and
   contain no alcohol imagery or explicit content, so were NOT flagged.
   - One of the three (`...d2e`, Open Bar) is doubly defective: the top line of "CHRISTMAS" is also
     cropped at the print-window edge, consistent with the known cropping pattern from earlier batches.

2. **Low-contrast/illegible text on black garment (5 products)** — recurrence of the color-authoring
   defect first found in batch §8m:
   - `6ac711ae57527ce5fd019716` "Christmas 2026 Caffeine Panic" — dark purple/green text on black.
   - `6ac7119d57527ce5fd01970b` "Avoiding Carolers Raccoon" — dark pink/green text on black.
   - `6ac7119957527ce5fd019707` "Avoiding Carolers Gnome" — same design family, same issue.
   - `6ac7117d3925bc76b9037258` "Merry Everything Happy Always" — silver/gray script on black.
   - (Chic Happens Cocktail, above, is counted once under brand-safety but is also low-contrast.)

3. **Minor text-layout defect (1 product):** `6ac7118407e4149a0f001011` "My Elf Made Me" — the two
   title lines ("MY ELF MADE" / "MADE ME DO IT") are spaced too close together and visually collide/
   overlap, reducing but not destroying legibility.

Clean (12): Christmas Mode Dnd, Christmas Excuse Eat King, Christmas Eat No January (both V1 teal and V2
black — V2 is a deliberately recolored/flattened file for dark-garment contrast, confirming the seller
has already identified and partially solved this exact color problem for at least one design), Christmas
Calories Dont Count, Calories Dont Count Thanksgiving, Bah Humbug Wine, Naughty List Resume, Naughty Is
New Nice, Christmas Lights Therapy, All I Want Nap, All I Want Nap Pillow.

**B) Wine Tumbler — 9/21 defective — NEW systemic finding, a legibility defect class distinct from the
established fabric print-window-cropping pattern:**

The Wine Tumbler's monochrome laser-engraving mockup style does not render all source artwork equally.
Simple, high-contrast flat-icon designs (a single wreath, a cross, a dove, a martini glass) engrave
perfectly. But designs with dense illustration or multi-line text frequently render as:
- **Washed-out / overexposed illustration** — the detailed artwork area bleeds into an indistinct silver
  blur while the artist clearly intended a recognizable scene: `6ac711c907e4149a0f001027` "Glory To God"
  (angel choir scene destroyed), `6ac7117f3925bc76b9037259` "Christmas Excuse Eat King" (crest/crown
  graphic destroyed), `6ac711bba005c19c550d896d` "Everyone Go Home" (house/tree scene destroyed).
- **Severe text garbling** — `6ac711c5c077332834034d1c` "Christ Is Born": the title overlaps into an
  unreadable mess and the crest below it is a blank silver smudge — the single worst-rendering product
  found in this entire recovery effort to date.
- **Moderate text bleed** (readable with effort, but visibly degraded) — `6ac711c2fb1d11d19e036fa9`
  "Blessed Christmas," `6ac711bec077332834034d18` "Amazing Grace," `6ac711adb81469132100375a` "Deck The
  Halls Sit Down," `6ac711a67be9df884b032d86` "Dear Santa Explain Santa."
- **Cropping** — `6ac711894bfa32451c054a74` "Christmas Open Bar" Wine Tumbler: same `openbar_fixed.png`
  file as the Youth Hoodie instance above, top line clipped by the tumbler's metallic rim in the mockup.

Clean (12): Dreaming Stress Free Christmas, Dear Santa Bank Account, Christmas Vacation Canceled,
Corkscrew Wine Repeat (notably: this SAME design was found CROPPED on the Lunch Box blueprint in an
earlier batch — clean here — reconfirming that cropping is a function of print-window size per blueprint,
not the artwork itself), Christmas Mode Dnd, Christmas Lights Therapy, Christmas Party Survivor,
Elegantly Wasted Martini, Eggnog Recovery Skeleton, Christmas Stress Tinsel Done, Christmas Wreath 2026,
Dear Santa Explain.

**C) Duvet Cover — 2/14 defective (both recurrences of known bad source files):**
- `6ac711a4b8d5d3e719013158` "Santa's Favorite Distraction" — filename
  `santas-favorite-distraction-FIXED3.png`. The ENTIRE product background renders as a gray/white
  transparency checkerboard. This confirms the checkerboard defect first found in an earlier batch
  (on a different design, also named "...FIXED3.png") is not a single bad file but a RECURRING AUTHORING
  WORKFLOW GAP — whatever process is supposed to flatten transparent PNGs before upload misses it
  inconsistently across multiple, unrelated designs.
- `6ac711c0a005c19c550d8971` "Tree Up Patience Down" — garbled "PATITANECE" text (should read PATIENCE),
  filename `flattened-tree-up-patience-down.jpg`. Recurrence of the known bad source file first found in
  batch §8i (previously confirmed on Mouse Pad, Napkin Set, Throw Pillow); this is at least its 4th
  confirmed occurrence across the Shopify catalog.

Clean (12): Avoiding Carolers Gnome, All I Want Nap, All I Want Nap Pillow, Too Lit Nice List, Survived
Christmas Hangover, Sleigh All Day, Santa's Favorite Customer VIP, Santa's Favorite Arrow, Santa I Can
Explain Clipboard, Prosecco Ho Ho Ho, Professional Gift Returner, Powered By Cookies.

**D) Cutting Board — 2/14 defective (both recurrences of known bad source files):**
- `6ac711ae57527ce5fd019715` "Happy Holidays Tree" — HAPPY text clipped at top edge
  (`nostalgic-happy-holidays-tree.jpg`), recurrence of the bad file first found in batch §8i (previously
  on Throw Pillow, Napkin Set, Tank Top, Coaster Set) — now confirmed on its 5th blueprint.
- `6ac711a0b8d5d3e719013155` "Candy Cane Lane" — banner area left blank, no text rendered
  (`nostalgic-candy-cane-lane.jpg`), recurrence of the bad file first found in batch §8i (previously on
  Coaster Set, Cloth Napkin Set) — now confirmed on its 3rd blueprint.

Clean (12): Noel Elegant, Merry Christmas Sant, Merry Bright Lights, Let It Snow, Joy To World, Joy Holly,
Jingle Bells, Home For Christmas, Fireplace Stocking, Deck The Halls, Christmas Wishes (Star), Believe
Sleigh, Peace On Earth, Seasons Greetings (13 listed — Cutting Board has 14 total, 2 defective, 12 clean;
list is illustrative, full accounting in the §23 ID table below).

**E) Winter Scarf — 0/21 defective.** Every design in this blueprint (a mix of faith/scripture-themed
and secular-humor titles) rendered cleanly: no cropping, no color-contrast issues, no garbled text. One
non-defect curiosity: `6ac711c3fb1d11d19e036faa` "Jingle All Way Liquor" has a title referencing
"Liquor" but the actual artwork is a plain "Jingle All The Way" car-with-gifts graphic with no alcohol
reference at all — likely SEO keyword stuffing in the title rather than a mismatched image, noted for
completeness but not counted as a visual defect.

**F) Shot Glass — 0/7 defective, all clean.**

**G) Shower Curtain — 0/3 defective, all clean.**

### Duplicate pairs: 0 confirmed
Full filename+scale+position cross-check across all 100 products found no same-blueprint duplicate
pairs. Several designs are legitimately reused across different blueprints (e.g. the faith-themed
`faith-glory-to-god.png`, `faith-christ-is-born.png` etc. family appears on both Wine Tumbler and Winter
Scarf; `all-i-want-nap.png` appears on both Duvet Cover and Shower Curtain) — normal POD practice, not a
duplicate-listing defect.

### Sync-mapping status
0/100 products in this batch show a populated Printify `external` field via the bulk `include_full=true`
list-page endpoint used for this batch — consistent with a known tooling gap already noted in an earlier
batch (the bulk list endpoint does not reliably surface this field), **not** a claim that these 100
products are actually unsynced to Shopify. As with every sync claim in this register, a populated field
would mean Printify-side mapping only, never confirmed Shopify-side buyability — the inverse (a null
field) carries even less evidential weight given the known tooling gap.

### Methodology note
All defect-rate figures in this register (here: 21%, and the 30/46/21/11/4/14/12/34/21/21% sequence
across the ten Shopify batches completed so far) are per-batch sample statistics, not an extrapolated
shop-wide prevalence — a point an independent reviewer correctly raised earlier in this effort and which
this register continues to apply. Each batch is a full 100/100 visual inspection of its own named ID
range, which is solid evidence for those specific products; it is not yet evidence for the ~1,069
Shopify products still unassessed.

## 8p. Shopify shop (26418699, Winstar4U) #1,361-1,460 — eleventh batch:
## COMPLETE: 100/100 visually inspected (42 in the first working session + 58 in a follow-up completion
## pass), 30/100 total confirmed defective, 2 true duplicate pairs, 1 ESCALATED brand-safety flag —
## alcohol/cocktail imagery confirmed on Baby Onesie (infant apparel), not just Youth Hoodie
## (2026-11-18, this session)

**Scope:** products ranked #1,361-1,460 by Shopify catalog list order in shop 26418699, enumerated via
`printify-get-products-9eb` list pages (positions 1,351-1,500) and sliced to the exact 100-product
range. Blueprint mix: Wine Tumbler (13), Winter Scarf (22), Shower Curtain (20), Baby Onesie (14), Shot
Glass (23), Youth Hoodie (4), Can Cooler (4). Bridge task T-163P2D4O6N (first 42) and T-47031X3I3F
(remaining 58 completion pass).

**COVERAGE NOTE:** the first working session individually viewed 42/100 and honestly flagged the
remaining 58 as NOT INDIVIDUALLY VIEWED THIS PASS rather than assuming them clean (see git history for
that intermediate state). A follow-up completion pass then viewed all 58 remaining mockups and replaced
every placeholder row in §24 with a real CLEAN/DEFECT result. This batch is now fully closed out at
100/100, consistent with #361-1,360.

### CONFIRMED DEFECTS: 30/100 total (12/42 from the first session + 18/58 from the completion pass — a
### batch-specific rate, not a shop-wide one, per the standing caveat)

**From the completion pass (58 products), 18 confirmed defects:**
- **Print-window cropping (11):** Shot Glass — `6ac71169336f8358660114b7` "Happy Holidays Tree" (HAPPY

  `6ac7112dfb1d11d19e036f50` "Jingle Bells Santa Smells" (minor top clip); Winter Scarf —
  `6ac71165f0a0ce97bc0c91d8` "Dear Santa Explain Santa" (minor top clip), `6ac7114a35a66ab7dc0b0f63`
  "Christmas Open Bar" (top line almost entirely cropped), `6ac7115435a66ab7dc0b0f67` "Christmas Vacation
  Canceled" (bottom line cropped), `6ac711516f976358160c470e` "Christmas 2026 Caffeine Panic" (top line
  almost entirely cropped), `6ac7113cc077332834034cca` + `6ac711386f976358160c46fe` "Christmas Eat No
  January" V1/V2 (bottom word "JANUARY" cropped on both — see duplicate-pair entry below).
- **Blank-banner-placeholder pattern (3, including 2 new designs beyond "Candy Cane Lane"):**
  `6ac71159ed135347800f9acc` "Candy Cane Lane" Shot Glass (7th occurrence), `6ac71126336f83586601148e`
  "Jingle All Way Liquor" Shot Glass (new design, same blank-banner defect), `6ac711357be9df884b032d51`
  "Truck Tree" Shower Curtain (new design, same blank-banner defect).
- **Known bad-source-file recurrences (2):** `6ac7113afb1d11d19e036f60` "My Elf Made Me" Shot Glass
  (duplicated word "MADE MADE", recurrence), `6ac7115a6356cae0df02e2e9` "Santas Favorite Customer VIP"
  Shower Curtain (literal "PRICE" placeholder text x3 + placeholder $99,999.99 total, recurrence).
- **New color-contrast defect (1):** `6ac7113450fc6704d30bf944` "Merry Everything Happy Always" Shot
  Glass — gray script text nearly illegible against the clear glass/white background (same design is
  legible on the white-garment Baby Onesie version — a substrate-specific legibility failure, same
  underlying lesson as the Wine Tumbler engraving defect).
- **Wine Tumbler engraving-legibility (1):** `6ac7114a83f1a0e50d071cfb` "Bah Humbug Wine" — engraving
  washes into illegible silver blur.

**Confirmed true duplicate pair (completion pass):** `6ac7113cc077332834034cca` "Christmas Eat No
January" and `6ac711386f976358160c46fe` "Christmas Eat No January V2", both Winter Scarf — identical
turkey-dinner artwork confirmed by direct visual comparison, re-exported under a different internal
filename. This is the SAME phrase/content pair already confirmed as a true duplicate on the Wine Tumbler
blueprint in the first 42 (`6ac7117b35a66ab7dc0b0f76`/`6ac7117857527ce5fd0196fd`) — the underlying design
has now been found duplicated on two separate blueprints.

**Brand-safety re-check (completion pass):** all 6 Baby Onesie products in the remaining 58 were viewed
specifically for alcohol/cocktail content given the standing escalated flag. **0 new instances found** —
none depict alcohol imagery. Several alcohol-themed designs (Prosecco, Corkscrew Wine, Christmas Open
Bar, Chic Happens Cocktail, a Shot Glass titled "Liquor" with no actual alcohol depicted) appeared only
on adult-appropriate blueprints (Shower Curtain, Winter Scarf, Shot Glass) and are explicitly NOT treated
as brand-safety issues — the flag is specifically about alcohol content printed on children's/infant
blueprints (Youth Hoodie, Baby Onesie), consistent with the existing finding below.

**Title/artwork word-mismatch, reconfirmed:** the Shot Glass titled "Jingle All Way Liquor" shows no
alcohol imagery at all (a wholesome Christmas road-trip station-wagon scene) — another instance of the
pattern already noted for "Bah Humbug Wine"/"Jingle All Way Liquor" Baby Onesie titles in the first 42:
titles carry a base design name that does not reliably predict actual artwork content in either
direction, so content-appropriateness checks must always be visual, never title-keyword-based.

### CONFIRMED DEFECTS FROM THE FIRST 42 VIEWED (unchanged from the original write-up)

**Wine Tumbler engraving-legibility defect (recurrence of the §8o defect class) — 4/13 viewed:**
- `6ac7117b35a66ab7dc0b0f76` "Christmas Eat No January" — text washes into illegible silver blur
- `6ac7117857527ce5fd0196fd` "Christmas Eat No January V2" — also illegible; **confirmed TRUE DUPLICATE**
  of the row above (same phrase/content, re-exported under a `flattened-...-v2.jpg` filename — direct
  visual comparison shows identical text content, not a different design)
- `6ac71152ed135347800f9ac9` "Checked Santas List" — dense small list-text illegible
- `6ac71159d0a23214d80b2571` is actually CLEAN (listed here to note it was checked, not a defect)

**Print-window cropping (established pattern, recurring on Winter Scarf/Baby Onesie/Can Cooler) — 7/42
viewed:**
- Winter Scarf: `6ac71176ed135347800f9ae0` "Elegantly Wasted Martini" (bottom line "SINCE 2026" cropped),
  `6ac7117afa3cae10170efa77` "Everyone Go Home" (top word clipped AND bottom line cropped)
- Baby Onesie: `6ac7115646dd1e896e04e0b6` "Avoiding Carolers Raccoon" (top word "CURRENTLY" clipped at
  collar), `6ac7114fc077332834034cdd` "All I Want Nap" (top line clipped at collar), `6ac711337be9df884b032d50`
  "Merry Christmas Lets Get Lit" (top word "MERRY" clipped — minor)
- Can Cooler: `6ac71132a005c19c550d893c` "Avoiding Carolers Raccoon" (top word clipped),
  `6ac7112e7be9df884b032d48` "Avoiding Carolers Gnome" (top clipped AND bottom cropped)

**Bad source-art-file recurrence — 1/42 viewed:**
- `6ac71179b814691321003745` "Tree Up Patience Down" Shower Curtain — the known "PATITANECE" garbled
  text (should read PATIENCE), its **5th confirmed occurrence** in this effort.

**New garbled-text defect:**
- `6ac7113d42c818a1ad095ed6` "My Elf Made Me" Baby Onesie — source art shows the word "MADE" rendered
  twice in sequence ("MY ELF MADE / MADE ME DO IT"), a duplicated-word rendering glitch distinct from the
  cropping and engraving-legibility defect classes already documented.

### ESCALATED BRAND-SAFETY FLAG (not resolved — owner review item)

`6ac7114807e4149a0f000fef` "Naughty List Skeleton V2" Christmas **Baby Onesie** — direct visual inspection
confirms the artwork shows a skeleton holding a martini/cocktail glass with visible purple liquid. The
same skeleton-with-cocktail design was already flagged as a brand-safety concern on the Youth Hoodie
(children's) blueprint in §8n/§8o; this batch confirms the identical design is ALSO mapped onto Baby
Onesie — infant apparel, an even younger-skewing product than Youth Hoodie. This raises the severity of
the standing finding: the whole "naughty/cocktail" design sub-family appears to be auto-applied across
every blueprint in the catalog, including infant clothing, with no content-appropriateness filter. **Not
resolved by this session — this is an owner-review item, consistent with the standing no-live-writes
rule.**

For contrast: the same skeleton-cocktail design on `6ac7114bc077332834034cd9`/`6ac71144a005c19c550d8945`
(Shot Glass, an inherently alcohol-adjacent adult barware product) is NOT a content-appropriateness issue
— only its appearance on children's/infant blueprints (Youth Hoodie, Baby Onesie) is being flagged.

### Title/artwork word-mismatch observations (not defects, informational)

Two Baby Onesie titles carry drink-related words ("Bah Humbug **Wine**", "Jingle All Way **Liquor**")
that do NOT correspond to any alcohol imagery in the actual artwork once viewed — both show innocuous
designs (a Scrooge/"Bah Humbug" illustration; a "Jingle All The Way" Christmas-Vacation station-wagon
illustration). This confirms titles carry a base design name across every blueprint it's printed on
(normal for this catalog's naming convention), which can read as alarming out of context but is not
itself evidence of inappropriate artwork — each instance must still be visually confirmed, which is why
this batch did not simply trust title keywords when assessing content-appropriateness.

### Sync-mapping status (batch, all 100, metadata-only, not sampled)

0/100 show a populated Printify `external` field via the bulk list endpoint. Per the standing register
caveat, this is Printify-side mapping-field evidence only — it does not confirm or deny actual Shopify
storefront status for any of these 100 SKUs.

### Methodology note

Duplicate-pair detection and defect classification followed the established method: print-area image
filename + scale + position + Printify blueprint match as the first-pass signal, with direct visual
comparison as ground truth for any candidate (filename matching alone is known from this effort to
produce both false positives and false negatives). All defect-rate and brand-safety-instance figures in
this section describe only the 42 products actually viewed in this pass, not the full 100.

## 8q. Shopify shop (26418699, Winstar4U) #1,461-1,560 — twelfth full-visual-inspection batch:
100/100 visually inspected, 26/100 confirmed defects, 2 NEW brand-safety-flag instances, 0 true
duplicate pairs (2026-11-18, this session)

**Status: COMPLETE — 100/100 individually viewed.**

### Defect tally by blueprint

| Blueprint | Defects | Total | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Baby Onesie | 4 | 17 | 2 brand-safety flags (alcohol imagery on infant apparel, NEW instances) + 2 minor top/edge-crop |
| Shot Glass | 7 | 21 | top/bottom-rim cropping — the cylindrical rim clips the first or last line whenever a design has 3+ stacked lines |
| Shower Curtain | 4 | 22 | overturns the earlier "always clean" assumption from smaller samples — top-line cropping + 2 more blank-banner recurrences |
| Can Cooler | 0 | 8 | reliably clean |
| Water Bottle | 7 | 11 | **NEW finding, highest defect rate of any blueprint in this effort (64%)** — text wraps around the bottle's cylindrical curvature and is cut off at the left/right seam on nearly every multi-word design |
| Winter Scarf | 6 | 14 | top/bottom-edge cropping, incl. one severe case (character almost entirely cropped off) |
| Fanny Pack | 0 | 7 | reliably clean |
| **Total** | **28 (26 defects + 2 brand-safety flags)** | **100** | |

### ESCALATED brand-safety finding — 2 NEW instances this batch

The alcohol-themed "Elegantly Wasted Martini" and "Eggnog Recovery Skeleton" designs (previously
confirmed on the Youth Hoodie blueprint) are now also confirmed on the **Baby Onesie** blueprint —
infant apparel. Both were directly viewed: one reads "ELEGANTLY WASTED SINCE 2026 / HOLIDAY
SPIRITS" next to a martini-glass graphic; the other reads "EGGNOG RECOVERY IN PROGRESS" on a
hangover-joke skeleton illustration. This brings the running brand-safety tally to **9 confirmed
instances across 2 blueprints** (6 Youth Hoodie + 3 Baby Onesie, including the 1 found in the
prior batch). This is a systemic catalog-mapping issue — the same small set of adult-humor designs
appears to be applied to every blueprint in a product family, including children's and infant
items, with no content-appropriateness filter. **Owner-review item, not resolved — no live writes
made.**

Also noted (not a defect): 2 products in this batch (Baby Onesie and Shot Glass "Jingle All Way
Liquor") have "Liquor" in the title but show zero alcohol imagery once actually viewed (a wholesome
road-trip/station-wagon design) — reinforcing the standing lesson that title keywords alone cannot
substitute for direct visual inspection, in either direction.

### NEW systemic finding: Water Bottle cylindrical wraparound cropping

7 of 11 Water Bottle products (64%) have text cropped at the left/right seam where the design wraps
around the bottle's curved surface — the highest defect rate of any blueprint found in this entire
recovery effort. This is a distinct root cause from the flat print-window cropping seen on Backpack/
Throw Pillow/etc. — the Water Bottle's cylindrical geometry itself clips any text placed near the
horizontal edges of the print area, regardless of scale value. Designs with short, centered text (e.g.
"Naughty Is New Nice," "My Elf Made Me") render clean; designs with longer phrases or text near the
edges (e.g. "Merry Everything Happy Always," "I'll Be Home For Christmas Probably") are reliably cut.

### Confirmed true duplicate candidates checked and ruled OUT

The "Naughty List Skeleton" V1/V2 pair recurs on Shower Curtain, Can Cooler, Water Bottle, and Fanny
Pack in this batch. Per the precedent established earlier in this effort (V1 has string-lights draped
over the ribcage that V2 lacks), all occurrences were checked by direct visual comparison and
confirmed as genuinely distinct art, not true duplicates.

### Sync-mapping status (batch #1,461-1,560)

6/100 products show a populated Printify `external` field. Per the register's standing caveat, this is
Printify-side channel-mapping evidence only — it does not confirm or deny actual Shopify-side
buyability, inventory, or storefront visibility for the other 94.

### Methodology note

Defect rates in this register are per-batch samples, not an extrapolated shop-wide prevalence rate —
each batch is reported on its own terms (per the standing caveat raised by independent review).

## 8r. Shopify shop (26418699, Winstar4U) #1,561-1,660 — thirteenth full-visual-inspection
batch: 100/100 visually inspected, 49/100 confirmed defects (highest batch rate in this effort),
1 NEW brand-safety-flag instance, 1 confirmed duplicate-pair recurrence, 0 new true duplicates
(2026-11-18, this session)

**Status: COMPLETE — 100/100 individually viewed.**

### Defect tally by blueprint

| Blueprint | Defects | Total | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Water Bottle | 24 | 32 | **75% — confirms and worsens the prior batch's 64% finding on a 3x larger sample.** Nearly every design with text reaching toward the horizontal edges is cut by the bottle's cylindrical wraparound; only short/small/centered text survives. |
| Fanny Pack | 15 | 28 | **54% — overturns the earlier "reliably clean" assumption** (0/7 and 0/8 in smaller prior samples). The designs that fail are a specific generic asset family (filenames like `faith-*`, scale 0.9, reused across many blueprints); purpose-built `fannypack-v1`/`-t5` assets are reliably clean. |
| Baby Onesie | 1 | 15 | Low cropping-defect rate (this blueprint's y=0.3 positioning keeps most designs clear of the edges), but surfaced 1 NEW brand-safety instance and 1 duplicate-pair recurrence (see below). |
| Tank Top | 2 | 15 | Low defect rate (y=0.38 positioning also stays mostly clear of edges). |
| Wall Tapestry | 7 | 7 | **100% — the single worst-performing blueprint found in this entire five-shop recovery.** Every design has text cropped at the left and/or right edge (and sometimes top/bottom), indicating the artwork's aspect ratio does not match this blueprint's print area at all, catalog-wide. |
| Shot Glass | 0 | 2 | Clean (both short/centered single-line designs). |
| Shower Curtain | 0 | 1 | Clean. |
| **Total** | **49** | **100** | |

### Root-cause refinement: it's an asset family, not a blueprint

This batch happened to print the SAME small set of designs across 5 different blueprints (Water
Bottle, Fanny Pack, Baby Onesie, Tank Top, plus earlier batches' Youth Hoodie), which makes the
real pattern visible for the first time: a generic image-asset family (large scale, typically 0.9,
centered at x=0.5) crops on nearly every blueprint whose print window is tight relative to the
artwork's own canvas — cylindrical Water Bottle/Shot Glass (wraparound), the curved zipper-top
edge of Fanny Pack, and (catastrophically) Wall Tapestry's aspect-ratio mismatch — while the same
designs render cleanly on blueprints with a generously sized or better-positioned print area (Baby
Onesie at y=0.3, Tank Top at y=0.38, large flat surfaces). The defect is best understood as an
artwork-positioning/QA gap applied blueprint-by-blueprint, not an inherent flaw in any one product
type.

### NEW brand-safety instance (owner-review item, not resolved)

"I'm Just For The Open Bar" (cocktail-glass graphic) confirmed on Baby Onesie — infant apparel.
This is a 3rd distinct alcohol-themed design confirmed on this blueprint (after "Elegantly Wasted
Martini" and "Eggnog Recovery Skeleton" in the immediately prior batch). Running brand-safety
tally across this recovery effort: **10 confirmed instances across 2 blueprints — 6 on Youth
Hoodie, 4 on Baby Onesie.** No live writes made; flagged for owner review only.

Also noted (not a defect, not a flag): the same "Eggnog Recovery Skeleton" design appears on Shot
Glass and Tank Top in this batch — both adult-oriented product types, not a brand-safety concern
there.

### Confirmed duplicate-pair recurrence (not a new pair)

"Christmas Eat No January" / "...V2" — the same phrase/content pair already confirmed as a true
duplicate on Wine Tumbler and Winter Scarf in earlier batches — now also found on Baby Onesie (3rd
blueprint occurrence of this one duplicate pair). Not a new finding, just a recurrence.

### Sync-mapping status (batch #1,561-1,660)

10/100 products show a populated Printify `external` field. Per the register's standing caveat,
this is Printify-side channel-mapping evidence only.

### Methodology note

Defect rates in this register are per-batch samples, not an extrapolated shop-wide prevalence rate.

## 8s. Shopify shop (26418699, Winstar4U) #1,661-1,760 — fourteenth full-visual-inspection
batch: 100/100 visually inspected, 19/100 confirmed defects, 5 NEW brand-safety instances
(all Youth Hoodie), 0 confirmed true duplicate pairs (2026-11-18, this session)

| Blueprint | Defects | Total | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Wall Tapestry | 8 | 8 | **100% — confirms and extends the §8r finding (7/7) across a second, larger sample; the single worst blueprint in this entire five-shop recovery.** Every tapestry this batch had its title text clipped at a left, right, top, or bottom edge, mid-word — a catalog-wide aspect-ratio mismatch between the source art and this blueprint's print window. |
| Duffel Bag | 5 | 6 | **83% — NEW problem blueprint, not previously sampled at volume.** Text is clipped at the left and/or right edge (missing first/last characters), distinct from the handle straps that cosmetically cross the design in every Duffel Bag mockup photo (that overlap alone is not counted as a defect — only genuine edge-truncation is). |
| Fanny Pack | 5 | 14 | 36% — consistent with the recurring curved-edge cropping pattern established in prior batches (§8o-§8r). |
| Youth Hoodie | 1 | 30 | 3% cropping rate, but see brand-safety findings below — this was otherwise the cleanest apparel blueprint in the batch for print-window cropping. |
| Tank Top | 0 | 28 | Clean. |
| Women's Tank Top | 0 | 9 | Clean — confirms, again, that designs which crop on Wall Tapestry/Fanny Pack/Duffel Bag render fine on apparel blueprints with smaller, well-positioned print areas. |
| Sweatpants | 0 | 4 | Clean (small leg-placement print, well within the print window). |
| Shorts | 0 | 1 | Clean. |

**NEW brand-safety escalation — 5 new alcohol/cocktail-themed designs confirmed on Youth Hoodie (children's blueprint) in a single batch:**
- "Elegantly Wasted Martini" — "Elegantly Wasted Since 2026, Holiday Spirits," martini glass graphic
- "Eggnog Recovery Skeleton" — "Eggnog Recovery In Progress," skeleton holding a drink
- "Corkscrew Wine Repeat" — "Corkscrew, Wine, Repeat. It's Christmas.," wine bottle + corkscrew graphic
- "Christmas Open Bar" — "I'm Just For The Open Bar," cocktail glass graphic (also has a top-edge cropping defect)
- "Chic Happens Cocktail" — "'Chic Happens' Holiday Cocktail Hour," martini glass graphic

**Running brand-safety tally: 15 confirmed instances across 2 blueprints (11 Youth Hoodie + 4 Baby Onesie).** Owner-review item only — not resolved, not acted upon, flagged for owner decision only, per standing directive.

**Keyword-vs-visual-content lesson reconfirmed twice more this batch:** "Bah Humbug Wine" (both Youth Hoodie and Fanny Pack/Duffel Bag versions) shows only a Scrooge-style character — zero alcohol imagery despite "Wine" in the title. "Jingle All Way Liquor" (Tank Top, Wall Tapestry, Women's Tank Top, Sweatpants versions) again shows only a wholesome station-wagon road-trip scene — zero alcohol content despite "Liquor" in the title. Both consistent with prior-batch findings; visual confirmation remains mandatory.

No true duplicate pairs found this batch — all repeated designs appeared across *different* blueprints (normal POD reuse), never twice on the *same* blueprint.

## 8t. Shopify shop (26418699, Winstar4U) #1,761-1,860 — fifteenth full-visual-inspection
batch: 100/100 visually inspected, 44/100 confirmed defects, 0 confirmed true duplicate pairs, 0 new
brand-safety instances (tally stays 15: 11 Youth Hoodie + 4 Baby Onesie) (2026-11-18, this session)

Note: a prior attempt at this exact batch (same product range) was claimed but failed mid-run due to an
API rate limit with zero real work done (confirmed independently by chatgpt, P-1V3T145I29, and honestly
left OPEN rather than submitted). This is a full clean restart, not a continuation.

| Blueprint | Defects | Total | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Wall Tapestry | 24 | 25 | **96% — confirms worst-blueprint status on the largest single-batch sample yet (prior: 7/7, then 8/8).** The one clean exception ("Christmas Wreath 2026") has no title text at all — a pure wreath illustration that fits the print window with no cropping. Every text-bearing Wall Tapestry design in this batch had its title clipped at a left, right, top, or bottom edge, mid-word. |
| Duffel Bag | 20 | 22 | **91%.** The two clean exceptions were both short designs: "Naughty List Skeleton V2" (a cocktail-holding skeleton, no text) and "Rejoice Star" (a single short word that fits the print window, unlike multi-word phrases like "All I Want For Christmas" or "Merry Christmas Let's Get Lit" which consistently clip). |
| Shorts | 0 | 28 | **100% clean.** Small corner-placed graphic with ample margin — never crops regardless of design. |
| Sweatpants | 0 | 25 | **100% clean.** Same small corner-placed graphic pattern as Shorts. |

**Mechanistic refinement (new this batch):** the three clean exceptions on otherwise near-100%-defective
blueprints all share one trait — short or absent title text. This confirms the standing root-cause
hypothesis precisely: the defect is driven by source-art/text length relative to the blueprint's fixed
print window, not by the blueprint being categorically broken. A one-word or no-text design fits; a
multi-word phrase does not. This should directly inform any correction approach (e.g., a maximum safe
character count per blueprint, or resizing/repositioning rules) once the owner authorizes remediation.

**0 confirmed true duplicate pairs.** Filename+scale+position+blueprint matching found no duplicates
within this batch (confirmed by direct script, not just visual spot-check). Several designs (Rejoice
Star, Savior Born Manger, Thankful Grateful Blessed, etc.) appear on 3-4 different blueprints each —
normal POD product-line variation, not a duplicate-listing defect.

**0 new brand-safety instances.** Several alcohol-themed titles appear in this batch ("Jingle All Way
Liquor," "Elegantly Wasted Martini," "Corkscrew Wine Repeat," "Eggnog Recovery Skeleton") but all are on
adult-apparel/accessory blueprints (Shorts, Sweatpants, Wall Tapestry, Duffel Bag) — none on Youth Hoodie
or Baby Onesie. The running brand-safety tally (owner-review item, not resolved) stays at 15 (11 Youth
Hoodie + 4 Baby Onesie).

**Sync-mapping status:** 0/100 show a populated Printify `external` field via this batch's bulk list
calls — consistent with the known tooling gap already documented for this shop, not a claim about actual
Shopify-side sync status (standing caveat).

### Running Shopify total: 1,500/2,069 products not-previously-individually-inspected now fully visually
inspected across fifteen consecutive batches (#361-460 through #1,761-1,860).

## 8u. Shopify shop (26418699, Winstar4U) #1,861-1,960 — sixteenth full-visual-inspection
batch: 100/100 visually inspected, 43/100 confirmed defects, 0 confirmed true duplicate pairs (1 flagged
candidate, see below), 0 new brand-safety instances (tally stays 15: 11 Youth Hoodie + 4 Baby Onesie —
no Youth Hoodie or Baby Onesie products in this batch's blueprint mix) (2026-11-18, this session)

| Blueprint | Defects | Total | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| Wall Tapestry | 9 | 9 | **100% defective.** Every title-bearing design clipped at an edge, mid-word — consistent with all prior samples (7/7, 8/8, 24/25). |
| Duffel Bag | 16 | 22 | **73%.** The 6 clean exceptions were all short/no-text designs ("Glory To God," "Amazing Grace," "Elegantly Wasted Martini," "Eggnog Recovery Skeleton," "Corkscrew Wine Repeat," "Christmas Mode Dnd") — confirms the standing text-length-vs-print-window hypothesis. |
| Apron | 4 | 6 | **67%.** NEW defect sub-type found on 2 of the 4: a non-transparent white background patch visible as a visible rectangle against the gray apron fabric, on top of the usual text cropping. |
| Oven Mitts | 3 | 8 | **38%.** First full sample of this blueprint — a tiny printable pocket panel (the rest of the mitt is unprinted black quilted fabric), so even moderate text length crops. |
| Sweatpants | 5 | 18 | **28%.** Same designs that were clean on Shorts (e.g. "Christmas Stress Tinsel Done," "Christmas Open Bar") cropped here — confirms the defect is blueprint-specific even within similar garment families, not just phrase-length-specific. |
| Shorts | 4 | 19 | **21%.** 2 defects were a NEW sub-type: low-contrast dark-colored text on black fabric, barely legible ("Dreaming Stress Free Christmas," "Corkscrew Wine Repeat") — extends the known light/dark-garment text-contrast defect category to this blueprint. |
| Canvas Wall Art | 1 | 3 | 33% — one minor top-edge crop ("Christ Is Born"). |
| Magnet | 1 | 8 | 12.5% — a recurrence (2nd occurrence) of the known "My Elf Made Me" source-art bug (the word "MADE" renders twice), now confirmed on a second blueprint (previously seen elsewhere in this effort). |
| Journal | 0 | 7 | **100% clean.** Full-bleed rectangular cover layout with generous margin — consistently the most reliable blueprint found in this effort. |

**Mechanistic confirmation:** this batch directly tested the "same design, different blueprint" question
on an unusually large scale — many designs in this batch (e.g. "Christmas Eat No January," "Christmas
Mode Dnd," "Corkscrew Wine Repeat") appear on 4-6 different blueprints each. The same design is
simultaneously clean on Journal/Shorts/Magnet and defective on Wall Tapestry/Duffel Bag/Sweatpants —
reconfirming that the defect is driven by each blueprint's fixed print-window size relative to the
design's text length, not by anything wrong with the design file itself.

**Duplicate-pair flag (not conclusively confirmed):** "Christmas Eat No January" vs "...V2" on Wall
Tapestry (IDs `6ac70ed44bfa32451c0548a4` / `6ac70ed157527ce5fd01957a`) are visually near-identical in
composition (same turkey-dinner illustration, same framing) on direct side-by-side comparison, though
automated print-area filename+scale+position matching did not find an exact match (as in several prior
cases in this effort, filename matching alone is not fully reliable). Flagged for owner review rather
than asserted as confirmed, pending a closer pixel-level comparison. The equivalent V1/V2 pair on Duffel
Bag was checked and ruled OUT as genuinely distinct (different border/decoration framing: snowflakes vs.
wreath-and-stars).

**0 new brand-safety instances** — this batch's blueprint mix (Wall Tapestry, Duffel Bag, Oven Mitts,
Magnet, Journal, Apron, Canvas Wall Art, Shorts, Sweatpants) contains no Youth Hoodie or Baby Onesie
products at all, so no new check was possible. Running tally stays 15 (11 Youth Hoodie + 4 Baby Onesie).
Several alcohol-themed titles appear ("Bah Humbug Wine," "Elegantly Wasted Martini," "Corkscrew Wine
Repeat," "Christmas Open Bar") but all are on adult-apparel/accessory blueprints — not a brand-safety
concern on their own.

**Sync-mapping status:** 0/100 show a populated Printify `external` field via this batch's bulk list
calls — consistent with the known tooling gap already documented for this shop, not a claim about actual
Shopify-side sync status (standing caveat).

### Running Shopify total: 1,600/2,069 products not-previously-individually-inspected now fully visually
inspected across sixteen consecutive batches (#361-460 through #1,861-1,960).
